## Network Source · 代碼示例

**id**　f85963c66e855b3894-7

**english**　Network Source

**chapter**　
- Python
- ArcPy 函數
- 描述資料
- Describe 屬性
- 網絡資料集屬性

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/functions/network-source.html#properties

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/functions/network-source.html

**text**　# Name: NDSNetworkSourceProperties_ex01.py
# Description: Print the information about the network sources for the
#              network dataset

import arcpy
import sys

# Set workspace
arcpy.env.workspace = "C:/Data/SanFrancisco.gdb/Transportation"

#Create a Describe object from the network dataset
desc = arcpy.Describe("Streets_ND")

justify = 35
print("------- Network sources")

#Get all the network sources for the network dataset
sources = desc.sources
if not sources:
    print("%*s" % (justify, "(No network sources)"))
    sys.exit(0)

for source in sources:
    print("%*s: %s" % (justify, "Source Name" , source.name))
    print("%*s: %s" % (justify, "Source ID" , str(source.sourceID)))
    print("%*s: %s" % (justify, "Source Type", source.sourceType))
    print("%*s: %s" % (justify, "Element Type", source.elementType))
    print(" ")

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/functions/network-source.html#properties

## DisconnectUser · 討論

**id**　f8c34b772cad10f104-2

**english**　DisconnectUser

**chapter**　
- Python
- ArcPy 函數
- 地理資料庫管理

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/functions/disconnectuser.html#discussion

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/functions/disconnectuser.html

**text**　管理員可使用 DisconnectUser 函數斷開用戶與企業級地理資料庫的連接。 此函數用于補充 ArcGIS Pro 中的 地理資料庫管理 對話框。
DisconnectUser 函數必須使用連接到資料庫的管理連接。
如果非管理員用戶嘗試運行此函數，則函數失敗。
選擇除用于運行函數或資料庫的管理員連接之外的所有用戶，斷開其連接。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/functions/disconnectuser.html#discussion

- users
- Specifies the users who will be disconnected from the geodatabase. sde_id—Users will be disconnected based on the ID value returned from the ListUsers function. This can be passed to the function as an individual SDE ID value or a list containing multiple SDE ID values. ALL—All connected users will be disconnected. 注: This function will not disconnect the user who is running the function.
- Integer

**content_version**　3.7

## DisconnectUser · 代碼示例

**id**　f8c34b772cad10f104-9

**english**　DisconnectUser

**chapter**　
- Python
- ArcPy 函數
- 地理資料庫管理

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/functions/disconnectuser.html#code-sample

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/functions/disconnectuser.html

**text**　import arcpy

admin_workspace = "D:\\MyProject\\admin.sde"
arcpy.env.workspace = admin_workspace
user_name = "GDB"

# Look through the users in the connected user list and get the connection ID.
# Use the connection ID to disconnect the user that matches the username variable
users = arcpy.ListUsers(admin_workspace)
for item in users:
    if item.Name == user_name:
        arcpy.DisconnectUser(admin_workspace, item.ID)

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/functions/disconnectuser.html#code-sample

## DisconnectUser · 代碼示例

**id**　f8c34b772cad10f104-11

**english**　DisconnectUser

**chapter**　
- Python
- ArcPy 函數
- 地理資料庫管理

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/functions/disconnectuser.html#code-sample

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/arcpy/functions/disconnectuser.html

**text**　import arcpy

# Set the admistrative workspace connection
admin_workspace = "D:\\MyProject\\admin.sde"

# Create a list of users to disconnect.
user_names = ["TRAVIS", "DEBBIE", "PHIL"]

# Get a list of connected users
connected_users = arcpy.ListUsers(admin_workspace)

# Loop through the list of connected users and run DisconnectUser
# if the user name is in the user_names list
for user in connected_users:
    if user.Name in user_names:
        print('Disconnecting {0}'.format(user.Name))
        arcpy.DisconnectUser(admin_workspace, user.ID)

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/arcpy/functions/disconnectuser.html#code-sample
